# Puddle Island verification

**Dated entries** (what changed, what ran, what was not run) are one file each in [`docs/verification/`](verification/), named `<date>-<slug>.md`, so parallel sessions never edit the same lines (from 2026-11-12). `node scripts/verification.mjs new "Title (PR #n)"` makes one and `node scripts/verification.mjs latest` lists the newest. Entries from before 2026-11-12 are in dated files in `docs/archive/` (the earliest are [the archived log](archive/VERIFICATION-2026-10-07-13.md) and [the 2026-10-05/06 log](archive/VERIFICATION-2026-10-05-06.md)). This file holds only the coverage summary; `scripts/verification.test.mjs` fails `npm test` if a dated entry is added here.

## Coverage summary

One row per suite or area, overwritten with its latest pass (suite names and filters are in the [README](../README.md#browser-checks)); a row links to its entry file, or to an archived entry. Re-check this table before re-running a suite.

**Caveats** name what a session could have run and did not (offline, `world`, another level, a suite not re-run after a fix). They do not list iPad, phone, speech-on-a-device, controller or child checks: none of those has been done for any row, the line under the table says so once, and they are [parked](DEVICE-CHECKS.md) (2026-11-09). An older row or entry that still calls one "open" means parked.
